# Query plans (EXPLAIN ANALYZE, BUFFERS)

Generated by `world-library explain` (`make explain`) on a seeded database: ~1000000 books, ~200000 authors, PostgreSQL 17.12 (Debian 17.12-1.pgdg13+1).
Each query is the exact SQL produced by the application's query builders, executed with real parameter values (custom plan, `EXPLAIN ANALYZE`) and additionally planned as a **generic plan** (`EXPLAIN (GENERIC_PLAN)`, what a prepared statement may switch to after 5 executions). The check fails if either plan contains a `Seq Scan` on books, authors, book_authors, author_books, isbn_registry, audit_log (or their partitions), or if a list query uses OFFSET.

## Summary

| # | Query | Endpoint | Execution, ms | Seq Scan on big tables (custom + generic plan) |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| 1 | books: default sort (-created_at), first page | `GET /api/v1/books` | 2.12 | none ✅ |
| 2 | books: sort=title, second page (keyset) | `GET /api/v1/books?sort=title&cursor=…` | 1.36 | none ✅ |

## 1. books: default sort (-created_at), first page

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT $1
```

## 3. books: sort=-publication_year + year range

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books?sort=-publication_year&year_from=1990&year_to=2000`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.publication_year IS NOT NULL AND coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) >= $1 AND coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) <= $2 ORDER BY coalesce(b.publication_year, 32767::smallint) DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT $3
```

## 4. books: language filter + sort=title

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books?language=ja&sort=title`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.language_code = $1 ORDER BY b.title ASC, b.id ASC LIMIT $2
```

## 7. books: publisher filter

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books?publisher=…`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.publisher = $1 ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT $2
```

## 8. books: created_by filter

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books?created_by=…`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.created_by = $1 ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT $2
```

## 9. books: created_from/created_to

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books?created_from=2024-01-01&created_to=2024-01-08`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.created_at >= $1 AND b.created_at < $2 ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT $3
```

## 10. books: title substring (trigram)

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books?title=…`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND f_unaccent(b.title || ' ' || coalesce(b.original_title, '')) ILIKE '%' || f_unaccent($1) || '%' ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT $2
```

## 12. books: author name filter

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books?author=akhmatova`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM (SELECT DISTINCT ab.book_id FROM authors a CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT ab0.book_id FROM author_books ab0 WHERE ab0.author_id = a.id) ab WHERE a.deleted_at IS NULL AND f_unaccent(a.full_name || ' ' || coalesce(a.original_name, '')) ILIKE '%' || f_unaccent($1) || '%') ids CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT * FROM books b0 WHERE b0.id = ids.book_id LIMIT 1) b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT $2
```

## 13. books: author_id filter

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books?author_id=…`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.id IN (SELECT ab.book_id FROM author_books ab WHERE ab.author_id = $1) ORDER BY b.created_at DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT $2
```

## 19. authors: born range, sort=birth_date

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/authors?born_from=1800-01-01&born_to=1850-12-31&sort=birth_date`

```sql
SELECT a.id, a.full_name, a.original_name, a.birth_date, a.death_date, a.country_code, a.biography, a.external_ids, a.created_at, a.created_by, a.updated_at, a.updated_by, a.deleted_at, a.version FROM authors a WHERE a.deleted_at IS NULL AND a.birth_date IS NOT NULL AND coalesce(a.birth_date, 'infinity'::date) >= $1 AND coalesce(a.birth_date, 'infinity'::date) <= $2 ORDER BY coalesce(a.birth_date, 'infinity'::date) ASC, a.id ASC LIMIT $3
```

## 21. search: type=all, full-text mode (common word)

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/search?q=garden`

```sql
SELECT kind, id, label, rank FROM (SELECT 'book'::text AS kind, 1 AS kind_ord, b.id, b.title AS label, (b.tr + similarity(f_unaccent($1), f_unaccent(b.title || ' ' || coalesce(b.original_title, ''))))::float8 AS rank FROM (SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, ts_rank(b.search_vector, websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($2))) AS tr FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($3)) ORDER BY tr DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT 1000) b UNION ALL SELECT 'author'::text AS kind, 0 AS kind_ord, a.id, a.full_name AS label, (a.tr + similarity(f_unaccent($4), f_unaccent(a.full_name || ' ' || coalesce(a.original_name, ''))))::float8 AS rank FROM (SELECT a.id, a.full_name, a.original_name, ts_rank(a.search_vector, websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($5))) AS tr FROM authors a WHERE a.deleted_at IS NULL AND a.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($6)) ORDER BY tr DESC, a.id DESC LIMIT 1000) a) s WHERE ($7::float8 IS NULL OR (s.rank, s.kind_ord, s.id) < ($8::float8, $9::int, $10::bigint)) ORDER BY s.rank DESC, s.kind_ord DESC, s.id DESC LIMIT $11
```

## 22. search: type=all, full-text mode (rare name)

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/search?q=dostoevsky`

```sql
SELECT kind, id, label, rank FROM (SELECT 'book'::text AS kind, 1 AS kind_ord, b.id, b.title AS label, (b.tr + similarity(f_unaccent($1), f_unaccent(b.title || ' ' || coalesce(b.original_title, ''))))::float8 AS rank FROM (SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, ts_rank(b.search_vector, websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($2))) AS tr FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND b.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($3)) ORDER BY tr DESC, b.id DESC LIMIT 1000) b UNION ALL SELECT 'author'::text AS kind, 0 AS kind_ord, a.id, a.full_name AS label, (a.tr + similarity(f_unaccent($4), f_unaccent(a.full_name || ' ' || coalesce(a.original_name, ''))))::float8 AS rank FROM (SELECT a.id, a.full_name, a.original_name, ts_rank(a.search_vector, websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($5))) AS tr FROM authors a WHERE a.deleted_at IS NULL AND a.search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', f_unaccent($6)) ORDER BY tr DESC, a.id DESC LIMIT 1000) a) s WHERE ($7::float8 IS NULL OR (s.rank, s.kind_ord, s.id) < ($8::float8, $9::int, $10::bigint)) ORDER BY s.rank DESC, s.kind_ord DESC, s.id DESC LIMIT $11
```

## 23. search: type=books, fuzzy mode (typo)

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/search?q=Silnet Gardn`

```sql
SELECT kind, id, label, rank FROM (SELECT 'book'::text AS kind, 1 AS kind_ord, b.id, b.title AS label, word_similarity(f_unaccent($1), f_unaccent(b.title || ' ' || coalesce(b.original_title, '')))::float8 AS rank FROM books b WHERE b.deleted_at IS NULL AND f_unaccent($2) <% f_unaccent(b.title || ' ' || coalesce(b.original_title, ''))) s WHERE ($3::float8 IS NULL OR (s.rank, s.kind_ord, s.id) < ($4::float8, $5::int, $6::bigint)) ORDER BY s.rank DESC, s.kind_ord DESC, s.id DESC LIMIT $7
```

## 26. book card: by id

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books/{id}`

```sql
SELECT b.id, b.title, b.original_title, b.subtitle, b.description, b.isbn_10, b.isbn_13, b.language_code, b.publication_year, b.publisher, b.page_count, b.genres, b.edition, b.external_ids, b.created_at, b.created_by, b.updated_at, b.updated_by, b.deleted_at, b.version FROM books b WHERE b.id = $1 AND b.deleted_at IS NULL
```

```text
Index Scan using books_p00_pkey on books_p00 b (actual time=0.004..0.004 rows=1 loops=1)

  Buffers: shared hit=3
```

## 27. book card: authors of books (page enrichment)

Endpoint: `GET /api/v1/books/{id}`

```sql
SELECT ba.book_id, ba.author_id, a.full_name, ba.role, ba.ordinal FROM unnest($1::bigint[]) AS x(id) CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT * FROM book_authors b2 WHERE b2.book_id = x.id) ba CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT a2.full_name FROM authors a2 WHERE a2.id = ba.author_id AND a2.deleted_at IS NULL LIMIT 1) a ORDER BY ba.book_id, ba.ordinal, ba.author_id, ba.role
```
